124	            "question": "How does compile execute — in-process on the control-plane service? Why is --no-cpu-throttling --min-instances 1 required?",
125	            "answer": "Compile runs IN-PROCESS on the control-plane service, NOT dispatched to the L4 GPU Job. A kind='compile' job is claimed by db.claim_due_job in the in-process drain loop (job_worker.py:225) and executed by _run_claimed_job -> _main._execute_compile (job_worker.py:111-119). _execute_compile (orchestration.py:685-755) runs the ffmpeg reel stitch locally via _main.VideoCompiler.compile_highlights (lines 713-730) on the control-plane box; there is no remote dispatch in the compile path. The drain loop is a background ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) (job_worker.py:42-52); _drain_due_jobs loops claim+run until nothing runnable (job_worker.py:224-234), and parked/waiting jobs are re-driven by a threading.Timer wake (_arm_wake_timer, job_worker.py:254-266) — there is NO central scheduler; the DB is the clock. On Cloud Run this background thread only advances while the instance has CPU allocated. --no-cpu-throttling keeps the CPU allocated between requests so the drain thread actually finishes the stitch after /api/compile has already returned 202; without it the reel stalls and never completes (deploy/cloudrun/README.md:73,78-79). --min-instances 1 keeps one always-warm instance so the drain runs reliably and a scale-to-zero doesn't strand queued compile jobs (README.md:74). Defense-in-depth: recover_stale_jobs() at startup RE-QUEUES a compile interrupted by a restart/scale-down (idempotent CPU stitch) rather than failing it, so an unlucky mid-stitch scale-down self-heals on the next drain (database_jobs.py:407-437; called at main.py:1451; README.md:80-82). Process (GPU) jobs are instead marked failed since re-running could double-spend.",

### 45. assistant (2026-07-04T01:51:55.306Z)

The workflow returned a comprehensive map and surfaced **several corrections to the handoff plan** — critically, one potential crash-loop. Let me capture the key findings:

**Deploy-critical:**
1. **The `cloud-serving` preset (`presets.py:51-63`) forces `skip_ai_handoff=False`** (Gemini ON) — but our config.json sets it `true`. If the preset *overrides* explicit config, the CP would hit `AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING` and **crash-loop on boot** (no Gemini key). I must resolve preset-vs-config precedence before deploying.
2. The preset also sets `deployment.compute_target=cloud_run` (needed for dispatch) and `storage.backend=gcs` (satisfies `STORAGE_INCOHERENT`). So we *want* the profile — but need `skip_ai_handoff=true` to win.
3. **`/api/download/{name}` does not exist** — it's `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}`; and `/api/compile` requires an `output_filename` field (handoff CUJ omitted it). Both `/api/process` and `/api/compile` are async 202 → poll `/api/jobs/{id}`.
4. **Upload gap CONFIRMED**: uploaded local `/tmp` path → `_maybe_dispatch_remote` hard-fails ("cloud-serving needs a cloud input") — no auto-staging. Step-5 browser CUJ will fail by design → real bug.
5. **`DEPLOY_REPORT_cloud-serving_2026-07-04.md` already exists** (the #457 batching runlog) — must NOT overwrite; use a distinct filename.

The precedence question is a crash risk, so let me read the actual source rather than trust summaries, and check the runtime service account's IAM in parallel.

### 46. user (2026-07-04T01:51:55.830Z)

1	"""Deployment-profile presets + environment auto-detection (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M1).
2	
3	A *deployment profile* is a single switch that applies a coherent BUNDLE of existing
4	config knobs, so the truly-local / cloud-serving / cpu-mock modes don't drift apart one
5	knob at a time (the design in ``docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/README.md`` §4.3).
6	
7	Precedence (lowest → highest), realised by the loader::
8	
9	    built-in model defaults  <  profile preset  <  config.json  <  env  <  worker --set
10	
11	This module is **pure data + pure functions** — no IO, and (critically) **no torch import at
12	module load**. The loader applies a preset ONLY when a profile is EXPLICITLY selected
13	(``config.json`` ``deployment.profile`` or the ``DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`` env var); with no
14	profile selected the loader is byte-identical to the pre-M1 behaviour (``profile=""`` ==
15	today). Auto-detect only ever **suggests** a profile — it never auto-applies one (D15: cloud
16	spend is never entered silently).
17	"""
18	
19	from __future__ import annotations
20	
21	import copy
22	import os
23	from typing import Any, Mapping, Optional
24	
25	# Canonical enum values, defined ONCE here. ``models.DeploymentConfig``'s ``Literal`` mirrors
26	# these and ``tests/test_config_deployment.py`` pins parity so the two can never drift.
27	PROFILES: tuple[str, ...] = ("truly-local", "cloud-serving", "cpu-mock")
28	COMPUTE_TARGETS: tuple[str, ...] = ("local_gpu", "cloud_run", "cpu_mock")
29	
30	# Each profile maps 1:1 to its canonical compute_target. Used to apply the preset and to warn
31	# when config.json explicitly overrides compute_target into a state inconsistent with the profile.
32	COMPUTE_TARGET_FOR_PROFILE: dict[str, str] = {
33	    "truly-local": "local_gpu",
34	    "cloud-serving": "cloud_run",
35	    "cpu-mock": "cpu_mock",
36	}
37	
38	# A preset is a partial, nested override dict over the model defaults. It touches ONLY knobs
39	# that EXIST today — M1 is pure/additive: the new ``deployment.{profile,compute_target}`` plus
40	# ``indexing.{detector_impl,skip_ai_handoff}`` and ``storage.backend``. Later milestones extend
41	# these bundles — ``input_backend`` (M2) and the configurable output/workspace base (M3,
42	# Launch-Report-gated) — deliberately NOT set here so M1 never pre-empts a gated flip.
43	PROFILE_PRESETS: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {
44	    "truly-local": {
45	        "deployment": {"profile": "truly-local", "compute_target": "local_gpu"},
46	        # detector_impl stays "auto" (the runner picks WSL vs native by env on the GPU box).
47	        # Zero-cloud / privacy-first → run fully local with no Gemini handoff.
48	        "indexing": {"skip_ai_handoff": True},
49	        "storage": {"backend": "local"},
50	    },
51	    "cloud-serving": {
52	        "deployment": {"profile": "cloud-serving", "compute_target": "cloud_run"},
53	        # Linux L4 (cuda); Gemini handoff ON until the owned model clears the #172 floor (D11).
54	        # wasb GPU-feed tuning: the parity defaults (batch 8, no prefetch) left the paid L4
55	        # <1% utilized on the first real serving run (2026-07-03) — the pipeline was
56	        # CPU-feed-bound. Serving opts into the measured fast values; local/WSL unchanged.
57	        "indexing": {
58	            "detector_impl": "native",
59	            "skip_ai_handoff": False,
60	            "wasb": {"batch_size": 64, "prefetch_batches": 4},
61	        },
62	        "storage": {"backend": "gcs"},
63	    },
64	    "cpu-mock": {
65	        "deployment": {"profile": "cpu-mock", "compute_target": "cpu_mock"},
66	        # Deterministic CPU mock — no GPU/WSL, no provider/API. The CI / regression profile.
67	        "indexing": {"detector_impl": "stub", "skip_ai_handoff": True},
68	        "storage": {"backend": "local"},
69	    },
70	}
71	
72	
73	def is_known_profile(profile: str) -> bool:
74	    """True for a non-empty, recognised profile name."""
75	    return bool(profile) and profile in PROFILE_PRESETS
76	
77	
78	def preset_for(profile: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
79	    """Return a deep COPY of the preset bundle for ``profile`` (so callers can't mutate the
80	    module-level table), or ``{}`` for ``""`` / an unknown profile."""
81	    return copy.deepcopy(PROFILE_PRESETS.get(profile, {}))
82	
83	
84	def _truthy(val: Optional[str]) -> bool:
85	    """Common env-var truthiness (``1/true/yes/on``, case/space-insensitive)."""
86	    if val is None:
87	        return False
88	    return val.strip().lower() in {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
89	
90	
91	def _cuda_available() -> bool:
92	    """Best-effort CUDA probe. torch is heavy + optional, so it is imported lazily here and
93	    ONLY when a caller opts into ``probe_cuda``; any import/runtime failure means 'no GPU'."""
94	    try:
95	        # Intentionally lazy + local: keeps the config-load path torch-free (torch is heavy
96	        # and optional). Only reached when a caller opts into probe_cuda.
97	        import torch
98	
99	        return bool(torch.cuda.is_available())
100	    except Exception:
101	        return False
102	
103	
104	def probe_cuda_available() -> bool:
105	    """Public best-effort CUDA probe (lazy torch import; ``False`` on any failure).
106	
107	    Exposed for the per-profile startup checks (``backend/config/startup.py``) so a caller
108	    that *wants* the GPU heads-up can pass the result in without the startup module importing
109	    torch itself. Best-effort by design: if torch lives only in a detector subprocess env
110	    (native WASB), this returns ``False`` in the main process — which is why the startup checks
111	    treat a GPU mismatch as a WARNING (the runner healthcheck is the authority), never a hard
112	    error that could false-fail."""
113	    return _cuda_available()
114	
115	
116	def suggest_profile(
117	    env: Optional[Mapping[str, str]] = None, *, probe_cuda: bool = False
118	) -> str:
119	    """SUGGEST (never auto-apply — D15) a deployment profile from the environment.
120	
121	    Signals, in priority order:
122	
123	      - ``K_SERVICE`` set (Cloud Run)        → ``"cloud-serving"``
124	      - ``CI`` truthy                        → ``"cpu-mock"``
125	      - ``probe_cuda`` and a CUDA GPU present → ``"truly-local"``
126	      - otherwise                            → ``""`` (no strong suggestion)
127	
128	    ``probe_cuda`` defaults to ``False`` so the common config-load path stays cheap and never
129	    imports torch; a CLI ``--detect`` or cloud-serving startup that wants the GPU probe passes
130	    ``probe_cuda=True``. The result is advisory only: the caller decides whether to act on it.
131	    """
132	    e = os.environ if env is None else env
133	    if e.get("K_SERVICE"):
134	        return "cloud-serving"
135	    if _truthy(e.get("CI")):
136	        return "cpu-mock"
137	    if probe_cuda and _cuda_available():
138	        return "truly-local"
139	    return ""
140

41	    replace). Used ONLY for the profile-preset precedence path so a config.json sub-key
42	    overrides a preset sub-key while sibling preset/default sub-keys survive — the no-profile
43	    path keeps the original shallow ``{**defaults, **file_data}`` merge unchanged."""
44	    out = dict(base)
45	    for key, val in override.items():
46	        if isinstance(val, dict) and isinstance(out.get(key), dict):
47	            out[key] = _deep_merge(out[key], val)
48	        else:
49	            out[key] = val
50	    return out
51	
52	
53	def _resolve_explicit_profile(file_data: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
54	    """The EXPLICITLY chosen deployment profile, env winning over config.json. ``""`` if none
55	    is set. Auto-detect is deliberately NOT consulted here — it only suggests (D15), so a bare
56	    environment never silently selects a profile (and never silently spends on cloud).
57	
58	    An *unrecognised* ``DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`` env value warns and FALLS THROUGH to the file's
59	    profile (rather than suppressing it) — so an env typo can't leave the recorded profile
60	    asserting a preset that was never applied. A bad value in config.json is left to surface as
61	    a hard, typed-Literal error downstream (config-file correctness)."""
62	    env_profile = os.environ.get("DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE")
63	    if env_profile and env_profile.strip():
64	        ep = env_profile.strip()
65	        if is_known_profile(ep):
66	            return ep
67	        logger.warning(
68	            "[CONFIG] unrecognized DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE env value %r (valid: %s); ignoring it "
69	            "and falling back to config.json deployment.profile.",
70	            ep,
71	            ", ".join(PROFILE_PRESETS),
72	        )
73	        # fall through to the file's profile (env typo must not suppress a valid file choice)
74	    dep = file_data.get("deployment")
75	    if isinstance(dep, dict):
76	        prof = dep.get("profile")
77	        if isinstance(prof, str) and prof.strip():
78	            return prof.strip()
79	    return ""
80	
81	
82	def _unknown_key_paths(
83	    data: dict[str, Any], model_cls: type[BaseModel], prefix: str = ""
84	) -> list[str]:
85	    """Dotted paths in `data` that the typed config will not honor.
86	
87	    Two silent failure modes exist without this check: a typo'd TOP-LEVEL key is
88	    kept by AppConfig's ``extra="allow"`` but never read by typed code, and a
89	    typo'd key INSIDE a section is silently dropped by Pydantic. Either way the
90	    user's intent is lost — collect every such key so load_config can warn.
91	    """
92	    unknown: list[str] = []
93	    fields = model_cls.model_fields
94	    for key, val in data.items():
95	        if key not in fields:
96	            unknown.append(prefix + key)
97	            continue
98	        ann = fields[key].annotation
99	        if (
100	            isinstance(val, dict)
101	            and isinstance(ann, type)
102	            and issubclass(ann, BaseModel)
103	        ):
104	            unknown.extend(_unknown_key_paths(val, ann, f"{prefix}{key}."))
105	    return unknown
106	
107	
108	# Cached model defaults — Pydantic field defaults are class-level constants
109	# that never change at runtime. Initialised lazily on first call to
110	# _get_builtin_defaults() so importing this module is still cheap.
111	_BUILTIN_DEFAULTS_CACHE: dict[str, Any] | None = None
112	
113	
114	def _get_builtin_defaults() -> dict[str, Any]:
115	    """Return a plain dict of the current built-in defaults.
116	
117	    The AppConfig model is the single source of truth for defaults.
118	    Result is cached — Pydantic field defaults never change at runtime.
119	    (The live config returned by load_config() is NOT cached — it is
120	    a fresh AppConfig instance every call, reflecting config.json/env.)
121	    """
122	    global _BUILTIN_DEFAULTS_CACHE
123	    if _BUILTIN_DEFAULTS_CACHE is None:
124	        _BUILTIN_DEFAULTS_CACHE = AppConfig().model_dump(mode="json")
125	    return _BUILTIN_DEFAULTS_CACHE
126	
127	
128	def load_config(path: str | Path | None = None) -> AppConfig:
129	    """Load configuration with the following precedence:
130	
131	    1. Built-in defaults defined in the Pydantic models.
132	    2. Values from the JSON file (if present and readable).
133	    3. (Future) environment / CLI overrides.
134	
135	    Missing file or unreadable file → returns a fully defaulted AppConfig.
136	    Unknown keys in the file are tolerated (extra="allow" on the model).
137	    """
138	    cfg_path = Path(path) if path is not None else default_config_path()
139	
140	    file_data: dict[str, Any] = {}
141	    if cfg_path.exists():
142	        try:
143	            with open(cfg_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
144	                file_data = json.load(f)
145	        except Exception as exc:
146	            # Non-fatal: continue with defaults + whatever we could parse.
147	            # In production we might log here.
148	            logger.warning(
149	                f"Failed to read {cfg_path}: {exc}. Using defaults + partial data."
150	            )
151	
152	    # A config.json that is valid JSON but not an OBJECT (e.g. `[]`, `42`, `"x"`, `null`) would
153	    # otherwise crash startup: a truthy non-mapping hits `.items()` in _unknown_key_paths, and any
154	    # non-mapping breaks the `{**defaults, **file_data}` unpack. Degrade to defaults + warn, per the
155	    # loader's "bad input is non-fatal" contract.
156	    if not isinstance(file_data, dict):
157	        logger.warning(
158	            "%s is not a JSON object (got %s); ignoring it and using defaults.",
159	            cfg_path,
160	            type(file_data).__name__,
161	        )
162	        file_data = {}
163	
164	    if file_data:
165	        unknown = _unknown_key_paths(file_data, AppConfig)
166	        if unknown:
167	            logger.warning(
168	                "[CONFIG WARNING] %s contains keys the typed config does not "
169	                "recognize (typo'd or removed knobs — top-level extras are kept "
170	                "but unread; unknown section keys are silently dropped): %s",
171	                cfg_path,
172	                ", ".join(sorted(unknown)),
173	            )
174	
175	    # Precedence: built-in defaults < profile preset < config.json (< env/--set, applied at
176	    # consumption sites downstream). A *deployment profile* is opt-in — it is applied ONLY when
177	    # explicitly selected (DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE env or config.json deployment.profile). With no
178	    # profile the merge is byte-identical to the pre-M1 loader, so the default path is unchanged.
179	    defaults = _get_builtin_defaults()
180	    profile = _resolve_explicit_profile(file_data)
181	
182	    if profile and not is_known_profile(profile):
183	        # Only an unrecognised value from config.json reaches here (env typos already fell back
184	        # in _resolve_explicit_profile). Warn + apply no preset; the bad value also hits the
185	        # typed Literal at model_validate below, a hard clear error — loud, never silent.
186	        logger.warning(
187	            "[CONFIG] unrecognized deployment profile %r (valid: %s); applying no preset.",
188	            profile,
189	            ", ".join(PROFILE_PRESETS),
190	        )
191	        profile = ""
192	
193	    if profile:
194	        merged = _deep_merge(_deep_merge(defaults, preset_for(profile)), file_data)
195	        # Record the profile actually applied — the env may have chosen it over config.json,
196	        # so re-stamp it onto the merged deployment section to keep the record honest.
197	        merged.setdefault("deployment", {})
198	        if isinstance(merged["deployment"], dict):
199	            merged["deployment"]["profile"] = profile
200	            ct = merged["deployment"].get("compute_target")
201	            canonical = COMPUTE_TARGET_FOR_PROFILE.get(profile)
202	            if not ct and canonical:
203	                # An active profile with no explicit compute_target (e.g. config.json blanked it
204	                # to "") gets its canonical target back — the record never silently lies about an
205	                # active profile's compute_target (it stays meaningful for the M2+ ComputeBackend).
206	                merged["deployment"]["compute_target"] = canonical
207	            elif ct and canonical and ct != canonical:
208	                logger.warning(
209	                    "[CONFIG] deployment profile %r normally uses compute_target=%r but "
210	                    "config.json overrides it to %r — honoring the explicit override.",
211	                    profile,
212	                    canonical,
213	                    ct,
214	                )
215	            logger.info(
216	                "[CONFIG] deployment profile ACTIVE: %s (compute_target=%s)",
217	                profile,
218	                merged["deployment"].get("compute_target"),
219	            )
220	    else:
221	        # No profile → same deep merge so a config.json sub-key overrides its
222	        # default while NEW defaults added in the model since the file was written
223	        # survive — no silent loss of new knobs on upgrade (D4).
224	        merged = _deep_merge(defaults, file_data)
225	        suggestion = (
226	            suggest_profile()
227	        )  # cheap, env-only (no torch); SUGGESTS only (D15)
228	        if suggestion:
229	            logger.debug(
230	                "[CONFIG] no deployment.profile set (manual knobs). Environment suggests %r — "
231	                "set deployment.profile or DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE to apply (auto-detect never "
232	                "auto-applies — D15).",
233	                suggestion,
234	            )
235	
236	    return AppConfig.model_validate(merged)
237	
238	
239	def save_default_config(path: str | Path | None = None) -> Path:
240	    """Write a fresh default config.json.
241	
242	    Used by scripts/doctor.py diagnostics and the --setup CLI path.
243	    Overwrites if the file exists.
244	    """
245	    cfg_path = Path(path) if path is not None else default_config_path()
246	    cfg = AppConfig()
247	    cfg_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
248	    cfg_path.write_text(cfg.model_dump_json(indent=4), encoding="utf-8")
249	    return cfg_path

So our `indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true` beats the preset's `False` → no `AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING` crash. The preset still supplies `compute_target=cloud_run` + `storage.backend=gcs` (satisfies `STORAGE_INCOHERENT`) + wasb batch tuning, none of which config overrides.

1	# Cloud Run GPU **Job** image for the rally worker (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M6/M7, ADR-012/D16).
2	#
3	# Runs `python -m backend.worker infer …` INLINE on an L4 (detect + window + stitch co-located so
4	# CPU/wall + egress are metered into one job — D4). Built + pushed + verified by the owner on the
5	# M7 real run (the $100/₹10k budget, D17); CI does NOT build this (a CUDA image is too heavy) — the
6	# dispatch shim is proven with a fake client (tests/test_compute_backend.py).
7	#
8	# Two python stacks, deliberately separate (a framework conflict otherwise — same split as the
9	# native GPU box, deploy/digitalocean/gpu_setup.sh):
10	#   * the MAIN app env (this repo + ffmpeg) drives the pipeline + stitching;
11	#   * a conda `wasb` env (py3.8 + torch 1.11.0+cu113) runs the WASB shuttle detector as a subprocess
12	#     (the native runner shells out to $WASB_PYTHON), so the app's torch never co-installs with it.
13	#
14	# ⚠ WASB-C3: the pretrained badminton weights are academic-data-trained — provenance NOT cleared for
15	#   commercial sharing. They are NOT baked into this image; stage them at runtime (a bucket fetch or a
16	#   mount) and point $WASB_WEIGHTS at them. Resolve WASB-C3 before any public, non-owner serving.
17	
18	FROM nvidia/cuda:12.4.1-runtime-ubuntu22.04
19	
20	ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive \
21	    PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
22	    PIP_NO_CACHE_DIR=1 \
23	    CONDA_DIR=/opt/conda \
24	    WASB_REPO_DIR=/opt/models/WASB-SBDT
25	
26	# ffmpeg (stitch/decode) + DejaVu fonts (the watermark filter) + git/curl for the WASB env build.
27	RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
28	        ffmpeg fonts-dejavu-core git curl ca-certificates build-essential \
29	        python3 python3-pip python3-venv \
30	    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
31	
32	# --- WASB detector env (micromamba + conda-forge — BSD-licensed, NO Anaconda ToS; P3a, ADR-005) ---
33	# Enforces ADR-005 (every dependency — code, data, weights, AND the build toolchain — stays
34	# MIT/Apache/BSD): micromamba (BSD-3) + conda-forge (community) replaces Miniconda's Anaconda-ToS-gated
35	# `defaults` channels (the ≥200-employee Terms-of-Service trap flagged in PACKAGING_PLAN.md §3).
36	# MAMBA_ROOT_PREFIX=$CONDA_DIR (=/opt/conda) keeps the env at $CONDA_DIR/envs/wasb so $WASB_PYTHON below
37	# is UNCHANGED. Same verified torch-1.11.0+cu113 WASB stack; the pip wheels are unaffected by the switch.
38	ENV MAMBA_ROOT_PREFIX=/opt/conda
39	RUN curl -sSL https://micro.mamba.pm/api/micromamba/linux-64/latest \
40	        | tar -xj -C /usr/local bin/micromamba \
41	    && micromamba create -y -q -n wasb -c conda-forge python=3.8 \
42	    && git clone --depth 1 https://github.com/nttcom/WASB-SBDT.git "$WASB_REPO_DIR" \
43	    && "$CONDA_DIR/envs/wasb/bin/python" -m pip install -q \
44	        torch==1.11.0+cu113 torchvision==0.12.0+cu113 --extra-index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu113 \
45	    && ( [ -f "$WASB_REPO_DIR/requirements.txt" ] && "$CONDA_DIR/envs/wasb/bin/python" -m pip install -q -r "$WASB_REPO_DIR/requirements.txt" || true ) \
46	    && "$CONDA_DIR/envs/wasb/bin/python" -m pip install -q \
47	        hydra-core omegaconf pandas opencv-python-headless pillow scipy tqdm pyyaml matplotlib "numpy<1.24"
48	
49	# --- Main application env ---
50	WORKDIR /app
51	COPY pyproject.toml README.md ./
52	COPY requirements-trackers.txt ./
53	COPY backend ./backend
54	COPY training ./training
55	# Install with the [storage-gcs] extra: the cloud worker PULLS the gs:// input and PUSHES the
56	# gs:// outputs/done-marker through backend.storage (google-cloud-storage is lazy-imported there).
57	# Without it the job dies at the first gs:// fetch with `ModuleNotFoundError: google.cloud` — the
58	# real-Cloud-Run failure the mock-client tests can't see (the bucket I/O is faked in tests).
59	# D13/P12: `trackers` (ByteTrack's new home) is a git-only dep (broken PyPI wheel —
60	# roboflow/trackers#474) so it's NOT pulled by `pip install .`; install it out-of-band before
61	# the main env, or the person-cue path crashes with ModuleNotFoundError. The immutable git SHA is
62	# single-sourced in requirements-trackers.txt.
63	RUN python3 -m pip install --upgrade pip \
64	    && python3 -m pip install -r requirements-trackers.txt \
65	    && python3 -m pip install ".[storage-gcs]"
66	
67	# The native runner finds the WASB env + repo by env (no `conda activate` needed); weights come in at
68	# runtime (WASB-C3). detector_impl=native = the Linux-native WASB runner; cuda stays the default.
69	# PYTHONUNBUFFERED: without it the worker's stdout/stderr sit in the block buffer until process
70	# exit, so Cloud Logging shows a single end-of-run burst instead of live progress (observed on the
71	# first real M7 runs, 2026-07-03) — a running job looks stalled and mid-run failures log nothing.
72	ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 \
73	    WASB_PYTHON=/opt/conda/envs/wasb/bin/python \
74	    WASB_REPO_DIR=/opt/models/WASB-SBDT \
75	    WASB_WEIGHTS=/opt/models/WASB-SBDT/pretrained_weights/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar \
76	    RALLY_DETECTOR_IMPL=native
77	
78	# The dispatcher (CloudRunCompute) supplies the per-execution args:
79	#   infer --video-uri gs://… --out-uri gs://… --done-uri gs://… --segmenter wasb_hybrid --set …
80	ENTRYPOINT ["python3", "-m", "backend.worker"]
81

### 55. assistant (2026-07-04T01:57:13.995Z)

The Dockerfile resolves the key risk decisively:
- **The main app (fastapi/uvicorn/backend) is installed into the *system* `python3`** (`Dockerfile:63-65`), not the conda env. The `wasb` conda env is only a detector subprocess reached via `WASB_PYTHON`, "no conda activate needed" (line 67). So overriding `command` to `/bin/bash -c "... python3 -m backend.main --server ..."` runs the correct python with all deps. WORKDIR `/app` persists. `base64`/`bash`/`mkdir` are all in the ubuntu base. **The wrapper approach is safe.**

Given Windows `cmd.exe` re-parses `&&`/`|`/`>` inside gcloud args (the same class of bug that split my volume flag earlier), I'll deploy via a **`gcloud run services replace` YAML manifest** — the shell metacharacters live inside a file-read string and never cross the arg boundary. I'll generate it with a Python helper (reliable base64, no nested-quote hell) and verify the round-trip decode.

### 56. assistant (2026-07-04T01:57:29.546Z)

import base64, json

PROJECT = "gen-lang-client-0306026826"
REGION = "asia-southeast1"
IMAGE = f"{REGION}-docker.pkg.dev/{PROJECT}/rally/rally-worker:latest"
BUCKET = "gs://khelsutra-rally-serving"

# Explicit, crash-proof config. config.json wins over the cloud-serving preset (loader.py:194
# deep-merge: defaults < preset < config.json). We pin the crash-critical keys explicitly:
#   - indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true  -> beats preset False, so AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING never fires
#   - storage.backend=gcs            -> satisfies STORAGE_INCOHERENT under profile=cloud-serving
#   - deployment.compute_target=cloud_run -> ensures remote L4 dispatch is enabled
# The preset still supplies indexing.detector_impl=native + wasb batch64/prefetch4 (config doesn't override).
config = {
    "deployment": {"profile": "cloud-serving", "compute_target": "cloud_run"},
    "indexing": {"skip_ai_handoff": True},
    "storage": {
        "backend": "gcs",
        "output_root": BUCKET,
        "input_backend": "gcs",
        "input_root": f"{BUCKET}/videos",
    },
}
cfg_json = json.dumps(config, separators=(",", ":"))
b64 = base64.b64encode(cfg_json.encode()).decode()

# Round-trip verify
decoded = base64.b64decode(b64).decode()
assert json.loads(decoded) == config, "round-trip mismatch"

cmd = (
    "mkdir -p /tmp/apphome && "
    f"printf %s '{b64}' | base64 -d > /tmp/apphome/config.json && "
    "export RALLY_APP_HOME=/tmp/apphome && "
    "exec python3 -m backend.main --server --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8080"
)

yaml = f"""apiVersion: serving.knative.dev/v1
kind: Service
metadata:
  name: rally-control-plane
  namespace: {PROJECT}
  labels:

$svc = "https://rally-control-plane-2bosgeukpa-as.a.run.app"
$jid = "2a3032cd2e7140bcb66513eccba7d724"
$log = "C:\Users\avidu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-avidu-Projects-badminton-highlight-indexer\90c0b6b8-7430-4870-98cb-e487eacfa1d7\scratchpad\process_poll.log"
Set-Content -Path $log -Value "poll start for process job $jid"
for ($i = 0; $i -lt 120; $i++) {
    $tok = (gcloud auth print-identity-token 2>$null)
    $j = curl.exe -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $tok" "$svc/api/jobs/$jid" 2>$null
    $execs = (gcloud run jobs executions list --job rally-worker --region asia-southeast1 --format="value(metadata.name,status.runningCount,status.succeededCount,status.failedCount)" 2>$null) -join " | "
    $ts = Get-Date -Format "HH:mm:ss"
    $line = "[$ts] iter=$i exec=[$execs] :: $j"
    Add-Content -Path $log -Value $line
    if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"done"' -or $j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"failed"') {
        Add-Content -Path $log -Value "[$ts] TERMINAL state reached."
        break
    }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 25
}
Add-Content -Path $log -Value "poll loop exited at iter=$i"
Write-Output "DONE"
Get-Content -Path $log -Tail 3

4	Flow (the cloud-serving compute path):
5	  1. **Dispatch** a Cloud Run Job execution whose container runs ``python -m backend.worker infer
6	     --video-uri <gs> --out-uri <gs> --done-uri <gs> …`` (the SAME worker, on the L4 GPU).
7	  2. **Bucket-poll** ``done_uri`` via ``execution_policy.poll_until`` + the storage layer (D16 — the
8	     completion signal is a tiny marker object the worker writes, NOT the Cloud Run API status, so
9	     it is storage-backed and reuses the existing park/resume primitives). Scale-to-zero (D7): the
10	     cold-start is absorbed by the async poll. On a poll TIMEOUT (``max_wait_sec`` can undercut the
11	     job's own ``--task-timeout`` budget) the shim does ONE final marker check — a slow-but-healthy
12	     worker that finished just past the deadline is rescued, not wasted — then best-effort CANCELS
13	     the still-running execution so the L4 doesn't keep billing until ``--task-timeout``.
14	  3. **Read** the marker (it carries ``video_id`` + the worker's returncode) → fetch
15	     ``outputs/<video_id>/report.json`` → ``ComputeResult``.
16	
17	The dispatched container is forced to run **INLINE** (``compute_target=local_gpu`` + native detector)
18	so it never recursively re-dispatches — the dispatcher decides cloud, the container just computes.
19	
20	The Cloud Run trigger is an **injected client** (``CloudRunJobClient``) so the whole shim is unit-tested
21	with a fake (no GCP). The real ``GoogleCloudRunJobClient`` lazy-imports ``google-cloud-run`` and is
22	owner-verified on the M7 real run.
23	"""
24	
25	from __future__ import annotations
26	
27	import json
28	import logging
29	import os
30	import tempfile
31	import uuid
32	from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
33	from typing import Any, List, Optional
34	
35	from backend.compute.base import ComputeBackend, ComputeJobSpec, ComputeResult
36	from backend.infrastructure.execution_policy import (
37	    DEFAULT_POLICY,
38	    ExecutionPolicy,
39	    PolicyTimeout,
40	    poll_until,
41	)
42	from backend.storage import fetch, get_storage, parse_uri
43	
44	LOG = logging.getLogger("compute.cloud_run")
45	
46	# The container worker must run INLINE on the GPU — never re-enter the cloud-dispatch branch.
47	# (compute_target=local_gpu disarms the cmd_infer dispatch guard; native = the L4 WASB runner.)
48	_DEFAULT_CONTAINER_OVERRIDES: tuple[str, ...] = (
49	    "deployment.compute_target=local_gpu",
50	    "indexing.detector_impl=native",
51	)
52	
53	
54	class CloudRunJobClient(ABC):
55	    """Triggers a Cloud Run **Job** execution with per-execution container arg overrides.
56	
57	    Abstracted so ``CloudRunCompute`` is testable with a fake (no GCP). A real implementation
58	    overrides the job's container ``args`` for this execution and starts it."""
59	
60	    @abstractmethod
61	    def run_job(
62	        self,
63	        job_name: str,
64	        argv: List[str],
65	        *,
66	        region: str,
67	        project: Optional[str] = None,
68	    ) -> str:
69	        """Start a Cloud Run Job execution running ``python -m backend.worker <argv>``; return an
70	        execution id/name (for logs). Does NOT block on completion (the caller bucket-polls)."""
71	
72	    @abstractmethod
73	    def cancel_execution(self, execution: str) -> None:
74	        """Cancel a running execution by the name ``run_job`` returned. Called when the bucket-poll
75	        times out so the abandoned execution doesn't keep billing the GPU until ``--task-timeout``.
76	        May raise — the caller treats every failure as best-effort (the original poll timeout is
77	        NEVER masked by a cancel error)."""
78	
79	
80	class GoogleCloudRunJobClient(CloudRunJobClient):
81	    """Real client — lazy-imports ``google-cloud-run``. Owner-verified on the M7 real run; CI uses
82	    a fake, so this code path is never exercised in tests (the SDK isn't a test dependency)."""
83	
84	    def run_job(
85	        self,
86	        job_name: str,
87	        argv: List[str],
88	        *,
89	        region: str,
90	        project: Optional[str] = None,
91	    ) -> str:
92	        # A None/empty project would build a bogus "projects/None/locations/..." path (job_path
93	        # requires a real str). Fail loudly BEFORE the SDK import so the misconfig is exercisable
94	        # in CI (where google-cloud-run is absent) and so mypy narrows project to str for job_path.
95	        if not project:
96	            raise RuntimeError(
97	                "GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT is unset — the real Cloud Run dispatch needs a GCP project id "
98	                "to resolve the job path; set it on the control plane (see get_compute_backend)."
99	            )
100	        try:
101	            from google.cloud import run_v2  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
102	        except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - real SDK not present in CI
103	            raise RuntimeError(
104	                "google-cloud-run is required for CloudRunCompute's real client; install it on the "
105	                "control plane (it is intentionally NOT a CI/test dependency)."
106	            ) from exc
107	        client = (
108	            run_v2.JobsClient()
109	        )  # pragma: no cover - exercised only on the real M7 run
110	        name = client.job_path(project, region, job_name)
111	        overrides = run_v2.RunJobRequest.Overrides(
112	            container_overrides=[
113	                run_v2.RunJobRequest.Overrides.ContainerOverride(args=argv)
114	            ]
115	        )
116	        op = client.run_job(
117	            request=run_v2.RunJobRequest(name=name, overrides=overrides)
118	        )
119	        return (
120	            getattr(op, "metadata", None)

    "pytest",

# tests skip via pytest.importorskip. For local dev against a sibling checkout:

# express, so they are NOT declarable here. Install them out-of-band instead:
#   # CUDA 12.4 box:
#   pip install torch>=2.12.0 torchvision>=0.27.0 --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cu124
#   # CPU-only box (also what CI uses):
#   pip install torch torchvision --index-url https://download.pytorch.org/whl/cpu
gpu = []

# Storage backends (docs/DECOUPLED_COMPUTE/). The default `local` backend needs NONE of
# these. Cloud SDKs are imported lazily, so `import backend.storage` works without them;
# install the extra for the bucket you use. (boto3's S3 client covers AWS + Cloudflare R2 +
# DO Spaces + MinIO via endpoint_url.) `gdrive://` needs NO extra — it reads a locally
# MOUNTED Google Drive (Google Drive for Desktop) as plain files; see backend/storage/gdrive.py.

storage-gcs = ["google-cloud-storage>=2.16"]

$svc = "https://rally-control-plane-2bosgeukpa-as.a.run.app"
$jid = "d16654d1792b4a8ba266a0cef23e224c"
$dir = "C:\Users\avidu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-avidu-Projects-badminton-highlight-indexer\90c0b6b8-7430-4870-98cb-e487eacfa1d7\scratchpad"
$log = "$dir\process_poll.log"
$res = "$dir\process_result.json"
Set-Content -Path $log -Value "poll start for process job $jid"
$final = ""
for ($i = 0; $i -lt 160; $i++) {
    $tok = (gcloud auth print-identity-token 2>$null)
    $j = curl.exe -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $tok" "$svc/api/jobs/$jid" 2>$null
    $execs = (gcloud run jobs executions list --job rally-worker --region asia-southeast1 --format="value(metadata.name,status.runningCount,status.succeededCount,status.failedCount)" 2>$null) -join " ; "
    $ts = Get-Date -Format "HH:mm:ss"
    # short status extract
    $st = ""; if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"([a-z]+)"') { $st = $Matches[1] }
    $pg = ""; if ($j -match '"progress"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"') { $pg = $Matches[1] }
    Add-Content -Path $log -Value "[$ts] iter=$i status=$st progress=$pg exec=[$execs]"
    if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"done"' -or $j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"failed"') {
        $final = $j
        Set-Content -Path $res -Value $j
        Add-Content -Path $log -Value "[$ts] TERMINAL: $j"
        break
    }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 25
}
Add-Content -Path $log -Value "poll loop exited at iter=$i"
Write-Output "DONE terminal=$($final.Length -gt 0)"

for ($i = 0; $i -lt 12; $i++) {
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
    $t = (gcloud auth print-identity-token 2>$null)
    $j = curl.exe -s -H "Authorization: Bearer $t" "$svc/api/jobs/$pjid"
    if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"(done|failed)"') { Write-Output $j; break }
}

### 129. assistant (2026-07-04T02:20:45.193Z)

## Summary

Under `DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE=cloud-serving` (`deployment.compute_target=cloud_run`), the **browser-upload → process** CUJ fails end-to-end: an uploaded file lands on the **control-plane's local filesystem**, and `_maybe_dispatch_remote` **hard-rejects any local input** (the L4 Cloud Run Job cannot read a path local to the control-plane box). Nothing stages the uploaded file up to the bucket first, so the upload path is unusable in the hosted alpha.

The **gs:// bucket-path CUJ works** (verified on the deployed alpha 2026-07-04); only the browser-upload variant is broken.

## Runtime evidence (deployed alpha control plane, 2026-07-04)

The upload endpoints work and hand back a **local container path**:

```
POST /api/upload/init      -> {"upload_id":"…","max_part_mb":95,"next_part":"…/part/0"}
PUT  /api/upload/{id}/part/0 (raw bytes) -> {"received_parts":1,"received_bytes":18156}
POST /api/upload/{id}/complete -> {"path":"/tmp/apphome/output/uploads/tiny_test.mp4",
                                   "video_id":"…","size_bytes":18156,
                                   "next":"POST /api/process with this path"}
```

`POST /api/process {"video_path":"/tmp/apphome/output/uploads/tiny_test.mp4","compute":"cloud"}`
then fails — the input resolves to `backend=='local'`, which is rejected before dispatch.

## Code trace

- `backend/storage/ref.py:58-60` — a bare path parses to `backend='local'`.
- `backend/orchestration.py:250-255` — `_maybe_dispatch_remote`, on `input_ref.backend == "local"`, returns a **failed** job:
  > "cloud-serving needs a cloud input (e.g. gs://…); a path local to this machine can't be read by the Cloud Run job. Put the video in your bucket first."
- No `storage.put`/upload happens between local-detection and dispatch. `backend/storage/__init__.py:74-95` (`acquire_local_input`) only ever fetches **remote → local** (download); there is no **local → remote** staging helper wired into the dispatch or upload-complete path.

## Impact

A1 (alpha launch readiness) DoD requires "upload → process → jobs → compile → download". The hosted alpha satisfies this only via the gs:// path; the SPA/browser upload path 500s→failed-job under cloud-serving.

## Proposed fix

Stage the uploaded local file to `gs://<storage.input_root>/<video_id>/<filename>` before dispatch, then dispatch with that gs:// URI. Two candidate sites:

1. **`_maybe_dispatch_remote` (orchestration.py, just before the local-reject at :250)** — if the input is local **and** a cloud `storage.input_root` is configured, `storage.put(local → gs://input_root/<video_id>/<name>)` and continue with the gs:// URI. Most localized; keeps the upload endpoints backend-agnostic. **(preferred)**
2. **`/api/upload/{id}/complete` (backend/api/uploads.py:173-201)** — if `storage.input_backend` is non-local, put the assembled file to the bucket and return the gs:// URI as `path`.

Add a unit test mirroring `tests/test_compute_backend.py`'s dispatch tests with a fake storage `put` (verify a local input under cloud-serving is staged to `input_root` and dispatched as gs://, not rejected).

## Workaround (alpha)

Place the video in the serving bucket and call `POST /api/process {"video_path":"gs://…"}` directly — verified working on the deployed alpha.

## Related / adjacent gaps (already noted in code, not part of this issue)

- `player_pool` / `max_frames` are dropped on the cloud dispatch path (`orchestration.py:273` comment).
- Missing `google-cloud-run` in the worker image when reused as the control plane — tracked separately (deploy fix).

### 148. assistant (2026-07-04T02:23:43.888Z)

## What

Add a `cloud-run` optional-dependency (`google-cloud-run`) and install `.[storage-gcs,cloud-run]` in `deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile`, so the worker image — **when reused as the deployed control plane** — can dispatch inference to the L4 worker Job.

## Why (surfaced by the A1 alpha serving deploy)

`backend/compute/cloud_run.py`'s real client (`GoogleCloudRunJobClient`) lazy-imports `google.cloud.run_v2` to trigger the Cloud Run Job. The image installed only `.[storage-gcs]`, so on the **first real deploy of the control plane on Cloud Run** (2026-07-04), every `POST /api/process` with `compute: cloud` produced a failed job:

```
cloud dispatch error: google-cloud-run is required for CloudRunCompute's real client;
install it on the control plane (it is intentionally NOT a CI/test dependency).
```

The gs:// **input** path already worked (the CP pulled + validated the video from the bucket); only the **dispatch** step was missing its client. The worker Job itself runs inference inline and never dispatches, so it never needed the client — but the reuse-the-worker-image-as-control-plane design does.

## Changes

- `pyproject.toml`: new `cloud-run = ["google-cloud-run>=0.10,<1"]` extra (lazy-imported; **not** a CI/test dep — tests inject a fake `CloudRunJobClient`).
- `deploy/cloudrun/Dockerfile`: `pip install ".[storage-gcs,cloud-run]"` (+ comment explaining both extras).
- `tests/test_compute_backend.py`: the structural Dockerfile guard now asserts `.[storage-gcs,cloud-run]`.

## Verification

- `pytest tests/test_compute_backend.py` → **19 passed** (incl. the updated Dockerfile guard).
- `ruff check tests/test_compute_backend.py` → clean; `pyproject.toml` parses; `cloud-run` extra resolves (`google-cloud-run` built fine on Cloud Build).
- **Runtime**: after installing `google-cloud-run` on the deployed control plane, `/api/process` for a gs:// video successfully dispatched the L4 Job (`rally-worker` execution started, `progress: dispatching (cloud_run)`).

## Note on the live alpha

The alpha control-plane service is currently running a **derived image** (`rally-worker:latest` + `pip install google-cloud-run`) as an immediate unblock. Once this merges and the base worker image is rebuilt from the updated Dockerfile, the control plane can run the base `rally-worker` image directly (no derived image needed).

🤖 Generated with [Claude Code](https://claude.com/claude-code)

691	
692	    notify = progress or (lambda stage: None)
693	    params = json.loads(job.get("params") or "{}")
694	    video_id = job.get("video_id") or ""
695	    segment_ids = params.get("segment_ids") or []
696	    locale = params.get("locale")
697	
698	    notify("resolving")
699	    try:
700	        video, kept, out_name = _resolve_compile(
701	            db, cfg, video_id, segment_ids, params.get("output_filename", "")
702	        )
703	    except _CompileError as e:
704	        return {"status": "failed", "message": e.detail, "video_id": video_id}
705	
706	    stitching = getattr(cfg, "stitching", None) or StitchingConfig()
707	    output_dir = (
708	        getattr(getattr(cfg, "output", None), "output_dir", "output") or "output"
709	    )
710	    # Localize the branding watermark to the UI locale the SPA rendered in (recorded on the job);
711	    # unknown/absent locale keeps the configured default unchanged.
712	    localized_watermark = localize_watermark(stitching.watermark, locale)
713	    compiler = _main.VideoCompiler(
714	        output_dir,
715	        begin_padding=stitching.begin_padding,
716	        end_padding=stitching.end_padding,
717	        watermark=localized_watermark,
718	    )
719	    time_pairs = [(s["start_time"], s["end_time"]) for s in kept]
720	
721	    notify("stitching")
722	    local_src = ""
723	    try:
724	        # The stored `filepath` is the SOURCE ref (gs://, s3://, gdrive://, or a local path). Resolve to
725	        # a LOCAL file the stitcher can read — identity for a local path, fetch-to-scratch for a URI (the
726	        # same seam the process path uses); without it, compiling a bucket-ingested video can't open gs://.
727	        local_src = storage.acquire_local_input(
728	            video["filepath"], getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
729	        )
730	        out_path = compiler.compile_highlights(local_src, time_pairs, out_name)
731	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001 — a stitch/ffmpeg failure is a clean job FAILURE, not a crash
732	        logger.error(
733	            "[compile] stitching failed for video_id=%s: %s", video_id, e, exc_info=True
734	        )
735	        return {
736	            "status": "failed",
737	            "message": f"Highlight stitching failed: {e}",
738	            "video_id": video_id,
739	        }
740	    finally:
741	        if local_src:
742	            storage.cleanup_acquired_local_input(
743	                video["filepath"], local_src, getattr(cfg, "storage", None)
744	            )
745	
746	    # `filepath` is server-local (not browser-reachable); also return the download URL so the SPA needn't
747	    # hand-build it (P6 / PER_RALLY_SELECTION.md §4.3).
748	    download_url = f"/api/highlights/{quote(video_id)}/{quote(out_name)}"
749	    notify("finished")
750	    return {
751	        "status": "success",
752	        "filepath": out_path,
753	        "download_url": download_url,
754	        "video_id": video_id,
755	    }
756

    $ts = Get-Date -Format "HH:mm:ss"
    $st = ""; if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"([a-z]+)"') { $st = $Matches[1] }
    $pg = ""; if ($j -match '"progress"\s*:\s*"([^"]*)"') { $pg = $Matches[1] }
    Add-Content -Path $log -Value "[$ts] iter=$i status=$st progress=$pg"
    if ($j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"done"' -or $j -match '"status"\s*:\s*"failed"') {
        Set-Content -Path $res -Value $j
        Add-Content -Path $log -Value "[$ts] TERMINAL: $j"

    }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds 20

### 174. assistant (2026-07-04T02:31:19.159Z)

# A1 alpha serving deploy — hosted CUJ run report (cloud-serving)

**Date:** 2026-07-04 · **Region:** asia-southeast1 · **Project:** gen-lang-client-0306026826
**Scope:** A1 (ALPHA_LAUNCH_READINESS §7) — stand up the alpha serving endpoint and verify the
hosted `process → jobs → compile → download` CUJ against the WASB-only owned detector.

> Companion to `DEPLOY_REPORT_cloud-serving_2026-07-04.md` (PR #457, the GPU-batching runlog). That
> report proved the L4 worker + measured the batching win via the M6 dispatch **shim**; THIS report
> proves the deployed **control-plane service** end-to-end. They do not overlap.

## 1. What was deployed

| Resource | Value |
|---|---|
| L4 worker **Job** | `rally-worker` — nvidia-l4, 8 vCPU / 32Gi, `--no-gpu-zonal-redundancy`, `--max-retries 0 --task-timeout 3600`, GCS-FUSE mount `khelsutra-rally-serving`→`/gcs`, `WASB_WEIGHTS=/gcs/models/wasb_badminton_best.pth.tar` |
| Control-plane **Service** | `rally-control-plane` — rev 00002, `--no-cpu-throttling`, `min/maxScale 1/3`, cpu 2 / mem 4Gi, port 8080, **private (IAM-gated, no allUsers)** |
| Service image | `asia-southeast1-docker.pkg.dev/…/rally/rally-control-plane:latest` (= `rally-worker:latest` + `google-cloud-run`; see §5 bug 1) |
| Service URL | `https://rally-control-plane-2bosgeukpa-as.a.run.app` |
| Config (via base64 startup wrapper → `RALLY_APP_HOME=/tmp/apphome/config.json`) | `deployment.profile=cloud-serving`, `compute_target=cloud_run`, `indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true`, `storage.backend=gcs`, `output_root=gs://khelsutra-rally-serving`, `input_backend=gcs`, `input_root=…/videos` |
| Env | `DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE=cloud-serving`, `CLOUD_RUN_REGION=asia-southeast1`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT=…`, `RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS=*` (CLOUD_RUN_JOB is reserved → code default `rally-worker` is correct) |

**Boot-crash trap avoided:** the `cloud-serving` preset forces `indexing.skip_ai_handoff=False`
(Gemini ON), which would trip the `AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING` startup check (no Gemini key) and
crash-loop. Verified `loader.py:194` deep-merges `defaults < preset < config.json`, so the
explicit `skip_ai_handoff=true` wins. Also pinned `storage.backend=gcs` explicitly to satisfy the
`STORAGE_INCOHERENT` check. Boot log confirmed: `deployment profile ACTIVE: cloud-serving
(compute_target=cloud_run)` + `Uvicorn running on http://0.0.0.0:8080`.

## 2. Health / capabilities (authenticated via `gcloud auth print-identity-token`)

- `GET /api/health` → `200 {"status":"ok","sport":"badminton","default_segmenter":"yolo_hybrid"}`
- `GET /api/capabilities` → `segmenters` incl. `wasb_hybrid`; `deployment.cloud_available=true`;
  `gemini_key_present=false` (no crash — skip_ai_handoff honored); `gcs` backend usable.

## 3. gs:// bucket-path CUJ — PROVEN end-to-end

1. `POST /api/process {"video_path":"gs://khelsutra-rally-serving/videos/MahadevpuraSingles.MP4","segmenter":"wasb_hybrid","compute":"cloud"}` → `202 {job_id: d16654d1…}`
2. Poll `GET /api/jobs/{id}`: `validating → dispatching (cloud_run) → done`.
   - CP pulled + validated the video (format/resolution/pts/scene/blur/relevance all pass).
   - Dispatched L4 Job execution `rally-worker-k49kg`; worker log: `WASB in FULLY-LOCAL mode
     (skip_ai_handoff=True) … no gemini handoff, no API key needed`, `torch 1.11.0+cu113 cuda True`.
   - **Result: `success` — "Successfully indexed 48 play segments (cloud_run / 'wasb_hybrid')"**;
     outputs at `gs://khelsutra-rally-serving/outputs/915473…/`.
   - Wall: created 02:12:42 → finished 02:26:27 (**~13m45s**, incl. L4 cold image pull + inference).
3. `POST /api/compile {video_id, segment_ids:[1..48], output_filename:"mahadevpura_wasb_highlights.mp4"}` → `202 {job_id: d947a9e6…}`  <!-- COMPILE_RESULT -->
4. `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/mahadevpura_wasb_highlights.mp4` → <!-- DOWNLOAD_RESULT -->

## 4. Notes / observations

- **In-process compile serializes behind process jobs** (drain `ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)`):
  a queued compile waits while a process job holds the worker in its bucket-poll. Fine for the alpha
  (low concurrency); worth noting for load.
- **WASB batch tuning is NOT forwarded on the dispatch path.** `_maybe_dispatch_remote` forwards only
  `skip_ai_handoff` (orchestration.py:274-283); the L4 job runs its baseline batch (not the preset's
  batch64/prefetch4), so the measured #457 −30.5% win is not applied via the deployed CP. Minor
  (correctness unaffected); follow-up = forward `indexing.wasb.*` overrides like `skip_ai_handoff`.
- `player_pool` / `max_frames` are dropped on the cloud path (already noted at orchestration.py:273).

## 5. Bugs surfaced + handled

1. **`google-cloud-run` missing from the image** (BLOCKER). First real CP deploy failed every dispatch
   with *"google-cloud-run is required for CloudRunCompute's real client"* — the image installed only
   `.[storage-gcs]`; the reused-as-control-plane role needs `google.cloud.run_v2`. **Unblocked** with a
   derived image (`rally-worker` + `pip install google-cloud-run`). **Durable fix: PR #462** (adds a
   `cloud-run` extra + `.[storage-gcs,cloud-run]` in the Dockerfile + test guard; 19 tests pass).
2. **Browser-upload → gs:// staging gap.** Uploads land on the CP's local FS (`/api/upload/complete` →
   `/tmp/apphome/output/uploads/…`), and `_maybe_dispatch_remote` hard-rejects local input under
   cloud-serving (orchestration.py:250-255). Upload endpoints verified working; the gap is staging.
   **Filed: issue #461** (fix = stage local→gs://input_root before dispatch).

## 6. Auth posture + M9 (edge auth) — REMAINING

Service is currently **private** (Cloud Run IAM; reached in this run via owner identity token). A1 DoD
also wants **Cloudflare Access edge-auth ON** at `api.khelsutra.guru`. Prereqs discovered:
- Deployed image **lacks `PyJWT[crypto]`** (the `auth` extra) → enabling `edge_auth_enabled` would 500
  every gated request until another image rebuild (`.[storage-gcs,auth]`).
- Enabling edge-auth requires `security.cf_team_domain` + `cf_access_aud` + `allowed_video_root` all set
  or the server `sys.exit(1)`s on boot (startup.py exposure checks).
- Outward-facing: a CF Access application for `api.khelsutra.guru` (mirror the existing "KhelSutra
  Studio (alpha)" app), a DNS CNAME (Cloud Run domain-mapping is available in asia-southeast1), and an
  ingress lock to CF (exact mechanism not yet documented).

## 7. Cost ledger + teardown

- One L4 execution (~14 min, 8 vCPU + 1×L4) for the CUJ. CP service holds `min-instances 1` (small
  always-on CPU cost). Owner directive: **keep running** (closed alpha).
- Teardown (if needed): `gcloud run services delete rally-control-plane`, `gcloud run jobs delete
  rally-worker`, both `--region asia-southeast1`; bucket/secret/repo retained.

### 192. assistant (2026-07-04T02:38:01.937Z)

**Root cause confirmed: Cloud Run's ~32 MiB response-size limit.** Ranges transfer fully up to 29.6 MB (206) but ≥32.4 MB return 500. Starlette's `FileResponse` sets a fixed `Content-Length` (174 MB), so the full GET exceeds the cap. This is a real hosted-serving bug: any multi-rally reel >32 MiB can't be downloaded via `/api/highlights`.

To verify the **actual deliverable** is valid — and demonstrate the ranged-download workaround — let me reassemble the full 174 MB reel via <32 MiB Range chunks and ffprobe it.

### 197. assistant (2026-07-04T02:39:15.407Z)

## Summary

On the deployed Cloud Run control plane, `GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` returns **HTTP 500 (Google Frontend)** for any reel **larger than ~32 MiB**, even though the app itself returns 200 and the file is valid on disk. Every real multi-rally highlights reel exceeds 32 MiB, so the hosted **download** step of the CUJ is broken for normal output.

## Runtime evidence (deployed alpha, 2026-07-04)

A 48-rally reel (`mahadevpura_wasb_highlights.mp4`, **174,038,655 bytes / 166 MiB**, valid H.264 1080p30 + AAC, 244s) was compiled successfully (server log: `Dynamic highlight reel saved … (166.0 MB)`). Downloading it:

- Full `GET` → **500** from `server: Google Frontend`, `Content-Length: 0`; the app's own uvicorn access log shows **`200 OK`** for the same request (so the failure is at the Cloud Run edge, after the app returns 200 headers).
- `Range` probes pinpoint the boundary at exactly **32 MiB (33,554,432 bytes)**:

| Range requested | Result |
|---|---|
| `bytes=0-500000` (0.5 MB) | 206, full transfer |
| `bytes=0-31000000` (29.6 MB) | 206, full transfer |
| `bytes=0-34000000` (32.4 MB) | **500, 0 bytes** |
| `bytes=0-60000000` (57 MB) | **500, 0 bytes** |

## Root cause

`GET /api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}` returns a Starlette `FileResponse` (backend/main.py:1029-1057), which sets a **fixed `Content-Length`** equal to the file size. Cloud Run enforces a **~32 MiB limit on a single buffered HTTP/1 response**; a fixed-length 174 MB response is rejected at the edge with 500 after the app has already emitted 200 headers. (`cpu-throttling=false` is set and the in-process compile drain runs fine, so this is not throttling; and the app logs no exception.)

## Impact

A1 DoD requires "… → compile → **download** verified". The reel is produced correctly and is downloadable in <32 MiB **Range** chunks (verified: the 174 MB reel reassembled from 29 MB ranges ffprobes as a valid 244s H.264/1080p/AAC video), but a normal single `GET` from a browser/SPA fails for any real reel.

## Proposed fix

Serve large reels **out-of-band of the Cloud Run response body**:

1. **(preferred)** On compile, upload the reel to `gs://<output_root>/highlights/<video_id>/<name>` and have `/api/highlights/...` (and the compile job result `download_url`) issue a **redirect to a time-limited GCS signed URL** (`generate_signed_url`). The bytes then flow client↔GCS, never through the 32 MiB Cloud Run response path. This also fixes the ephemeral-`/tmp` problem (the reel currently only exists on the compiling instance).
2. **(fallback)** Stream without a fixed `Content-Length` (chunked `Transfer-Encoding`) — but the 32 MiB Cloud Run cap may still apply to buffered responses, so (1) is the robust fix.

## Workaround (verified)

Download via `Range` requests < 32 MiB and reassemble; the reassembled reel is byte-exact and plays.

## Related

Surfaced during the A1 alpha serving deploy. See also #461 (upload→gs:// staging) and #462 (cloud-run dispatch client). All three are hosted-serving gaps not visible in the local/single-box path.

# Session handoff — khelsutra-guru

> Last refreshed: 2026-07-04, A1 core serving DEPLOYED + gs:// CUJ PROVEN. See
> [[current-state]] + runlog PR #464.

## ▶ YOU ARE HERE — **A1 core is LIVE & proven; edge-auth (M9) is the last DoD item**

The alpha serving stack is **deployed and the gs:// CUJ works end-to-end**. A1 is **NOT flipped
DONE** yet — its DoD also needs edge-auth ON + the upload/download gaps closed (see bugs).

**Deployed (region asia-southeast1, project gen-lang-client-0306026826, KEEP running per owner):**
- L4 **Job** `rally-worker` (nvidia-l4, 8vCPU/32Gi, FUSE `khelsutra-rally-serving`→/gcs, `WASB_WEIGHTS=/gcs/models/…`).
- Control-plane **Service** `rally-control-plane` (rev 00002) — **PRIVATE (IAM, no allUsers)**,
  `--no-cpu-throttling` + `min/maxScale 1/3`, cpu2/mem4Gi. URL
  `https://rally-control-plane-2bosgeukpa-as.a.run.app`.
- Service image = **derived** `…/rally/rally-control-plane:latest` (= `rally-worker:latest` +
  `pip install google-cloud-run`; the base image lacked the dispatch client — PR #462 makes it durable).
- Config: base64 config.json via `RALLY_APP_HOME=/tmp/apphome` (built by
  `scratchpad/gen_service_yaml.py`); `profile=cloud-serving, compute_target=cloud_run,
  skip_ai_handoff=true, storage.backend=gcs, output_root/input_root=gs://khelsutra-rally-serving[/videos]`.
  Env: `DEPLOYMENT_PROFILE`, `CLOUD_RUN_REGION`, `GOOGLE_CLOUD_PROJECT`, `RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS=*`
  (⚠ `CLOUD_RUN_JOB` is RESERVED — code default `rally-worker` is correct).

**How to call it:** private → `curl -H "Authorization: Bearer $(gcloud auth print-identity-token)" <url>/api/health`.
Deploy via `gcloud run services replace scratchpad/rally-control-plane.service.yaml --region asia-southeast1`
(run gcloud from **PowerShell**, NOT git-bash — MSYS mangles `/gcs`; and comma-dict flags must be quoted).

**gs:// CUJ PROVEN (2026-07-04):** POST `/api/process {video_path: gs://…/MahadevpuraSingles.MP4,
segmenter: wasb_hybrid, compute: cloud}` → L4 job ran WASB (fully-local, skip_ai_handoff) → **48 rallies**
→ `/api/compile {video_id, segment_ids:[1..48], output_filename}` → **166 MiB 1080p30 H.264 reel,
244s, ffprobe-valid**. (Note: `/api/compile` needs `output_filename`; download is
`/api/highlights/{video_id}/{filename}`, NOT `/api/download/{name}`.)

## Next steps / open threads
1. **CF Access edge-auth (M9)** — the remaining A1 DoD item. **PENDING OWNER DECISION** (outward-facing
   production DNS + a CF Access app). Prereqs discovered: (a) deployed image LACKS `PyJWT[crypto]` →
   needs another image rebuild with the `auth` extra or edge-auth 500s every gated request; (b) enabling
   `security.edge_auth_enabled` REQUIRES `cf_team_domain`+`cf_access_aud`+`allowed_video_root` all set
   or the server sys.exit(1)s on boot; (c) DNS: Cloud Run domain-mapping IS available in asia-southeast1
   + CF CNAME + a CF Access app for `api.khelsutra.guru` (mirror "KhelSutra Studio (alpha)"); (d) ingress
   lock to CF is NOT documented (design it). CF token verified at `G:\My Drive\cloudflare.token.txt`.
   Full map: the Explore-agent output in the 2026-07-04 session; auth code = backend/api/auth.py,
   startup checks = backend/config/startup.py ~100-140.
2. **Merge PR #462** (google-cloud-run extra) once CI green → then the CP can use the base `rally-worker`
   image directly (drop the derived image).
3. **Fix issues #461 (upload→gs:// staging) + #463 (/api/highlights 32 MiB Cloud Run limit → signed URL).**
   Both block the full A1 DoD (upload path + browser download of real reels).
4. Then flip **A1 → DONE** in `docs/ALPHA_LAUNCH_READINESS.md`.

## Bugs FILED this session
- **PR #462** — add `cloud-run` extra + `.[storage-gcs,cloud-run]` Dockerfile (durable fix for the missing dispatch client; 19 tests pass locally).
- **Issue #461** — browser-upload local files not staged to gs:// before L4 dispatch (upload CUJ fails).
- **Issue #463** — `/api/highlights` 500s for reels >32 MiB (Cloud Run response cap vs FileResponse Content-Length; fix = gs:// signed-URL redirect). Workaround: ranged download.
- **PR #464** — this deploy's runlog (`docs/COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING/runlogs/DEPLOY_REPORT_cloud-serving_a1-cuj_2026-07-04.md`).

## Key decisions (don't relitigate)
- **WASB-only alpha** (`skip_ai_handoff=true`): deterministic, free, owned-model. Verified config.json wins
  over the cloud-serving preset's `skip_ai_handoff=False` (loader.py:194 deep-merge) → no boot crash.
- **Reuse worker image** for the CP (has FastAPI + all code). It needed `google-cloud-run` added (PR #462).
- **Kept the service PRIVATE (IAM)** for the smoke test instead of `--allow-unauthenticated` — an open
  endpoint that dispatches paid L4 jobs is a cost-abuse vector. CF Access is the intended tester-facing auth.
- **A1 NOT flipped DONE** — honest: edge-auth + #461/#463 remain. Core serving path is proven.

### 219. assistant (2026-07-04T02:45:51.013Z)

# Read CF token WITHOUT printing it. Only non-sensitive fields are echoed.
$t = (Get-Content "G:\My Drive\cloudflare.token.txt" -Raw).Trim()
$H = @{ Authorization = "Bearer $t"; "Content-Type" = "application/json" }
$api = "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"

function Get-CF($url) { return Invoke-RestMethod -Uri $url -Headers $H -Method GET }

Write-Output "=== token verify ==="
try { $v = Get-CF "$api/user/tokens/verify"; Write-Output ("status: " + $v.result.status) } catch { Write-Output "verify FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" }

Write-Output "`n=== accounts ==="
$acc = Get-CF "$api/accounts"
$acc.result | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ("account: {0}  id={1}" -f $_.name, $_.id) }
$acctId = $acc.result[0].id

Write-Output "`n=== zone khelsutra.guru ==="
$z = Get-CF "$api/zones?name=khelsutra.guru"
$z.result | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ("zone: {0}  id={1}  status={2}" -f $_.name, $_.id, $_.status) }
$zoneId = $z.result[0].id

Write-Output "`n=== Access org (team domain) ==="
try { $org = Get-CF "$api/accounts/$acctId/access/organizations"; Write-Output ("team/auth_domain: " + $org.result.auth_domain) } catch { Write-Output "org FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" }

Write-Output "`n=== Access apps (name / domain / aud) ==="
try {
  $apps = Get-CF "$api/accounts/$acctId/access/apps"
  $apps.result | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ("app: '{0}'  domain={1}  aud={2}" -f $_.name, $_.domain, $_.aud) }
} catch { Write-Output "apps FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" }

Write-Output "`n=== existing DNS records for api./app. ==="
foreach ($n in @("api.khelsutra.guru","app.khelsutra.guru")) {
  try {
    $d = Get-CF "$api/zones/$zoneId/dns_records?name=$n"
    if ($d.result.Count -eq 0) { Write-Output ("{0}: (none)" -f $n) }
    $d.result | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ("{0}: type={1} content={2} proxied={3}" -f $_.name, $_.type, $_.content, $_.proxied) }
  } catch { Write-Output "dns $n FAILED: $($_.Exception.Message)" }
}

$t = (Get-Content "G:\My Drive\cloudflare.token.txt" -Raw).Trim()
$H = @{ Authorization = "Bearer $t"; "Content-Type" = "application/json" }

$appId = "2bb7d8d7-e8df-4810-8fc4-40680e3aeb1f"
function TryGet($label, $url) {
  try { $r = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri $url -Headers $H -Method GET; Write-Output "OK  $label"; return $r }
  catch { Write-Output ("ERR $label -> {0}" -f $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__); return $null }
}
Write-Output "=== full existing app object (scan for team/issuer/domain) ==="
$app = TryGet "app" "$api/accounts/$acct/access/apps/$appId"
if ($app) { ($app.result | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6) -split "`n" | Select-String -Pattern "domain|aud|team|issuer|cloudflareaccess|self_hosted" }
Write-Output "`n=== probe team-domain endpoints ==="
TryGet "identity_providers" "$api/accounts/$acct/access/identity_providers" | Out-Null
$gc = TryGet "gateway/configuration" "$api/accounts/$acct/gateway/configuration"
$org2 = TryGet "organizations(account)" "$api/accounts/$acct/access/organizations"
if ($org2) { Write-Output ("auth_domain: " + $org2.result.auth_domain) }
Write-Output "`n=== try common team-name guesses against public certs endpoint (no auth) ==="
foreach ($team in @("khelsutra","khelsutra-guru","avidullu","avi-dullu")) {
  try { $c = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "https://$team.cloudflareaccess.com/cdn-cgi/access/certs" -Method GET -TimeoutSec 8; if ($c.keys) { Write-Output "FOUND team domain: https://$team.cloudflareaccess.com  (keys=$($c.keys.Count))" } }
  catch { Write-Output ("  $team.cloudflareaccess.com -> {0}" -f $_.Exception.Response.StatusCode.value__) }
}

$t = (Get-Content "G:\My Drive\cloudflare.token.txt" -Raw).Trim()
$H = @{ Authorization = "Bearer $t"; "Content-Type" = "application/json" }
$api = "https://api.cloudflare.com/client/v4"
$acct = "213c12cf60de9041dd8a3521aa206472"
$dir = "C:\Users\avidu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-avidu-Projects-badminton-highlight-indexer\90c0b6b8-7430-4870-98cb-e487eacfa1d7\scratchpad"

# Idempotency: if an app for api.khelsutra.guru already exists, reuse it.
$existing = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$api/accounts/$acct/access/apps" -Headers $H -Method GET
$app = $existing.result | Where-Object { $_.domain -eq "api.khelsutra.guru" } | Select-Object -First 1
if ($app) {
  Write-Output "reusing existing app for api.khelsutra.guru"
} else {
  $body = @{
    name = "KhelSutra API (rally-control-plane)"
    domain = "api.khelsutra.guru"
    type = "self_hosted"
    session_duration = "24h"
    app_launcher_visible = $false
  } | ConvertTo-Json
  $resp = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$api/accounts/$acct/access/apps" -Headers $H -Method POST -Body $body
  $app = $resp.result
  Write-Output "created app"
}
$appId = $app.id
$aud = $app.aud
Write-Output ("app id : {0}" -f $appId)
Write-Output ("app aud: {0}" -f $aud)

# Ensure the allowlist policy exists
$pols = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$api/accounts/$acct/access/apps/$appId/policies" -Headers $H -Method GET
if ($pols.result.Count -ge 1) {
  Write-Output ("policy already present: " + $pols.result[0].name)
} else {
  $polBody = @{
    name = "alpha allowlist"
    decision = "allow"
    include = @(
      @{ email = @{ email = "avi.dullu@gmail.com" } },
      @{ email = @{ email = "sheendullu13@gmail.com" } },
      @{ email = @{ email = "suneetadullu@gmail.com" } },
      @{ email = @{ email = "avidullu.svp@gmail.com" } }
    )
  } | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6
  $pr = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "$api/accounts/$acct/access/apps/$appId/policies" -Headers $H -Method POST -Body $polBody
  Write-Output ("policy created: " + $pr.result.name)
}
Set-Content -Path "$dir\cf_api_aud.txt" -Value $aud
Write-Output "saved aud -> cf_api_aud.txt"

45	def resolve_under_root(path: str, root: str) -> str:
46	    """Return the real path, asserting it is contained under `root` (raises ValueError if not)."""

No new app needed.

**The blocker (why I stopped before enabling edge-auth):**
- Enabling `edge_auth_enabled` **requires** `security.allowed_video_root` set or the server crash-loops on boot (`startup.py:115`).
- But `allowed_video_root` makes `/api/process` run `validate_input_path` → `resolve_under_root` → `os.path.realpath("gs://…")`, which mangles the gs:// URI into a local path and **rejects it as out-of-root** (`paths.py:45-55`, `main.py:912`). So **turning on M9 as-is breaks the gs:// CUJ I just proved.** The intended hosted-upload alternative is separately blocked by the #461 staging gap. Net: edge-auth + cloud-serving has no clean working input path without a code change.
- Also, `api.khelsutra.guru` is a **stale CNAME to a dead Cloudflare tunnel**; routing it to Cloud Run behind CF's proxy needs dashboard-level cert/Host setup (the managed-cert ACME challenge conflicts with CF proxying).

This is new information you didn't have when you said "do full M9" — so how do you want to proceed?

7	def output_base(config: Any) -> str:
8	    """The configured base dir for a segmenter's LOCAL scratch/staging artifacts
9	    (COMPUTE_DECOUPLED_SERVING M3). Returns ``output.output_dir`` (default ``"output"``) so the
10	    chunk-staging / AI-handoff / temp dirs follow ``--output-dir`` / ``output.output_dir`` instead of
11	    a hardcoded ``./output``. The DEFAULT is ``"output"`` → byte-identical to the prior literal.
12	
13	    This is the LOCAL output dir, deliberately NOT the storage ``workspace_root`` (which may be a
14	    ``gs://`` URI you can't ``makedirs``). Accepts the typed AppConfig or the legacy dict-shim
15	    (``.get("output", {}).get("output_dir")``); any miss or empty value falls back to ``"output"``."""
16	    try:
17	        out = (
18	            config.output.output_dir
19	            if not isinstance(config, dict)
20	            else config.get("output", {}).get("output_dir")
21	        )
22	    except Exception:
23	        out = None
24	    return out or "output"
25	
26	
27	def safe_output_filename(name: str) -> str:
28	    """Validate an output filename is a BARE name (no dir part, no traversal, not absolute).
29	
30	    `os.path.join(output_dir, name)` silently discards `output_dir` when `name` is absolute,
31	    and `..` segments escape it — so an unchecked `output_filename` is an arbitrary-write
32	    primitive. Returns the name unchanged when safe; raises ValueError otherwise.
33	    """
34	    if not name or not name.strip():
35	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not be empty")
36	    if "\x00" in name:
37	        raise ValueError("output_filename must not contain a null byte")
38	    if "/" in name or "\\" in name:
39	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must not contain a path separator: {name!r}")
40	    if os.path.isabs(name) or name != os.path.basename(name) or name in (".", ".."):
41	        raise ValueError(f"output_filename must be a bare filename, got: {name!r}")
42	    return name

12	#   - indexing.skip_ai_handoff=true  -> beats preset False, so AI_HANDOFF_KEY_MISSING never fires

## ✅ A1 — done for the session

I continued A1 from the handoff and took it end-to-end. The alpha serving stack is **live and proven**, and I found & handled every gap along the way.

**Deployed (asia-southeast1, owner said keep running):**
- **L4 worker job** `rally-worker` (nvidia-l4, 8 vCPU/32Gi) + **control-plane service** `rally-control-plane` (rev `00004`, `--no-cpu-throttling` + min-instances 1), private (IAM).

**gs:// CUJ proven end-to-end** — `POST /api/process` (gs:// video) → L4 ran WASB on the GPU (fully-local, no Gemini) → **48 rallies** → `/api/compile` → **valid 166 MiB, 1080p30 H.264, 244 s highlights reel** (I sent it to you above).

**M9 edge-auth ENABLED + gate verified** — `/api/process` without a CF JWT → **401**; `/api/health` → 200; boots cleanly.

**4 real hosted-serving bugs found — 2 fixed via PR, 2 filed:**
| # | What | Status |
|---|---|---|
| [PR #462](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/462) | image lacked `google-cloud-run` (blocked all dispatch) | fixed (durable) + worked around |
| [PR #465](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/pull/465) | `allowed_video_root` jail realpath-rejected gs:// (would've broken CUJ under edge-auth) | fixed + deployed |
| [#461](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/issues/461) | browser-upload not staged to gs:// | filed |
| [#463](https://github.com/Khelsutra/badminton-highlight-indexer/issues/463) | `/api/highlights` 500s for reels >32 MiB (Cloud Run cap) | filed |

## ⚠️ One thing to know about the current state

With **edge-auth ON + IAM-private**, the service now returns **401 to everyone (including you via identity-token) except `/api/health`** — it only becomes usable once it's reachable through Cloudflare Access. Two options:
- **Finish the DNS routing** (below) to make it tester-usable, **or**
- I can **roll it back to edge-auth-off** (owner-usable via identity token) until you're ready — just say the word (`EDGE_AUTH` unset → `services replace`).

## 📋 Your remaining M9 steps (the outward-facing DNS/CF bits I left to you)

CF Access is already set (the existing "KhelSutra Studio (alpha)" app covers `api.khelsutra.guru`). To route it:

2. Route `api.khelsutra.guru` → the service — Cloud Run domain-mapping (works in asia-southeast1) **or** a CF origin-rule Host-override to `rally-control-plane-2bosgeukpa-as.a.run.app`

### 330. assistant (2026-07-04T03:43:39.309Z)

Good question — it's the first, not the second. Here's the actual model of what we deployed:

## One ephemeral L4 Job **execution per video** (not a single always-on machine, not per-segment)

```
POST /api/process (video A)  ─┐
POST /api/process (video B)  ─┤   rally-control-plane          rally-worker (L4 GPU Job)
POST /api/process (video C)  ─┘   (1 always-warm CPU box)      scale-to-zero
                                         │  run_job()  ─────▶  execution for video A  ← whole video, all rallies
                                         │  run_job()  ─────▶  execution for video B
                                         │  run_job()  ─────▶  execution for video C
                                         │  (polls each done-marker in the bucket)
                                         └─ /api/compile → ffmpeg stitch runs HERE (CPU, in-process)
```

- **Segmentation = one Cloud Run Job execution per video.** Each `/api/process` fires exactly one `run_job()` (`backend/compute/cloud_run.py`) that runs `backend.worker infer` on the **entire** video (all ~13k frames → all 48 rallies in our test) on a fresh L4, writes `intervals.csv` / `report.json` to the bucket, then the box is torn down. So it's per-**video**, never per-segment.
- **The control-plane service is just the coordinator** — it dispatches jobs and bucket-polls for completion; it does **not** do the GPU segmentation. The only heavy CPU work it does itself is the reel **stitch** at `/api/compile` (in-process).
- **The L4 job scales to zero** between videos — you pay GPU only for each execution's runtime (~14 min for MahadevpuraSingles, cold pull included). That's the cost-efficient part: no idle GPU.

## One caveat worth knowing (current behavior)

Right now videos are processed **serially**, not in parallel — the control-plane's in-process job drain is single-threaded (`ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1)`), and the dispatching job holds that worker while it polls the L4 to completion. I observed this directly: a second `/api/process` stayed `queued` until the first finished. So today it's "one L4 execution at a time," even though Cloud Run Jobs *could* run many executions concurrently.

To fan out N videos in parallel later, the lever is bumping the drain's `max_workers` (and/or the L4 job's quota) — each would still be one execution per video, just overlapping. Want me to note that as a follow-up issue?

(Meanwhile, the `api.khelsutra.guru` cert is still provisioning in the background — I'll flip it to proxied + verify the moment it's ready.)
